The dataset and single-case evaluation instructions are available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/PKUAIWeb/SGR-BENCH.

</details>


### [220] [Evaluating Large Language Models as Live Strategic Agents: Provider Performance, Hybrid Decomposition, and Operational Gaps in Timed Risk Play](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22238)
*H. C. Ekne*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究在定时多阶段Risk环境中测试大语言模型（gemini-3.1-pro-preview、gpt-5.1、claude-opus-4-7、kimi-k2.6）的实时表现。32场冠军赛中Gemini赢得20场（p≈1.5×10⁻⁵），但将执行标准化为同一框架后差距消失（p≈0.821），说明差异来自端到端系统行为而非规划能力。机制分析显示Gemini更频繁关注终端目标且随胜利临近增强，并能将更多回合转化为深度征服链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态基准测试只能捕捉大语言模型行为的一部分，现实系统将模型置于包含时间限制、格式约束和失败模式的重复循环中，因此需要在更真实的动态环境下评估模型表现。

Method: 设计了一个定时多阶段Risk环境（含明确胜利目标及重复规划-执行循环），先进行跨提供商的32场冠军赛，再将执行层标准化为同一Gemini Flash脚手架，分离规划与执行能力进行对比，最后分析保存的规划与执行轨迹。

Result: Gemini-3.1-pro-preview在32场中赢20场（p≈1.5×10⁻⁵）；执行标准化后差异消失（p≈0.821）；Gemini更频繁引用终端目标且随胜利临近增加，并能将更多回合转化为深度征服链。

Conclusion: 实时智能体性能取决于目标追踪、执行转化率、成本和运行时可靠性，应把大语言模型评估为有界工作流中的组件而非孤立的基准测试应答者。

Abstract: Static benchmarks capture only part of how large language models behave in practice. Real systems place models inside repeated loops with time limits, formatting constraints, and failure modes. We study this setting in a timed multi-phase Risk environment with explicit victory targets and repeated planning and execution cycles. In a replicated 32-game cross-provider championship under frozen rules, gemini-3.1-pro-preview won 20 of 32 games against gpt-5.1, claude-opus-4-7, and kimi-k2.6, and the pooled winner distribution differs strongly from an equal-strength null (p approx 1.5 x 10^-5). We then separate planning from execution by standardizing execution on a cheaper Gemini Flash scaffold. Under this design, a pooled 32-game planner bakeoff is consistent with near-equality (p approx 0.821), which indicates that much of the earlier provider spread came from end-to-end system behavior rather than planning alone. To study mechanism, we analyze saved planning and execution traces from the provider championship. Gemini refers to the terminal objective far more often than the other models and increases that focus as victory approaches. Gemini also converts more turns into deep conquest chains, even though it is not the cleanest runtime. These results show that live-agent performance depends on objective tracking, execution conversion, cost, and runtime reliability, and they support evaluating LLMs as components in bounded workflows rather than as isolated benchmark respondents.

</details>


### [221] [Unlocking Proactivity in Task-Oriented Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22240)
*Hongbin Zhang,Ning Gao,Yuqin Dai,Ruiyuan Wu,Jinpeng Wang,Rena Wei Gao,Bingdong Tan,Shuzheng Gao,Zongjie Li,Chaozheng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向主动任务型对话（如外呼销售）的训练方法，通过认知用户模拟器建模用户潜在关注点，并利用非对称视角策略优化，使LLM从保守被动转变为能在有限轮次内主动引导用户接受的 persuasive 对话智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在主动任务型对话（如外呼销售）中表现保守被动，而传统奖励塑形RL（如GRPO）仅在已有被动策略的采样上重加权，无法从根本上激发主动能力。作者发现，利用用户的潜在关注点作为训练信号才能解锁真正的主动对话能力。

Method: (1) 构建认知用户模拟器（Cognitive User Simulator），将每个用户建模为分层面孔（可观测外部特征 + 隐藏内部关注点），生成忠实多样互动及每轮状态转换。(2) 提出模拟器诱导的非对称视角策略优化（Simulator-Induced Asymmetric-View Policy Optimization），包含两个目标：① 非对称在线自蒸馏，将具有关注点感知的特权视角行为迁移到仅依赖对话的可部署视角；② 状态转换策略细化。

Result: 未在摘要中明确给出具体数值结果，但表明该方法能有效解锁LLM的主动对话能力，使其在有限轮次内更好地说服用户。

Conclusion: 通过建模用户潜在关注点并在训练中将其作为关键信号，结合非对称视角策略优化，可以显著提升LLM在主动任务型对话中的说服力与效率。

Abstract: Proactive task-oriented dialogue (TOD), such as outbound sales, demands a persuasive agent that actively probes the user's concerns and steers the conversation toward acceptance within a bounded number of turns. Yet post-trained LLMs are inherently conservative, and reward-shaping RL (e.g., GRPO) struggles since it only re-weights what an already passive policy samples. We show that conditioning on the user's latent concerns unlocks proactive capability that no amount of sampling can undermine, establishing these concerns as a pivotal training-time signal. To operationalize this finding, we build the \textbf{Cognitive User Simulator}, which models each user as a stratified persona comprising observable external traits and hidden internal concerns. The simulator produces faithful and diverse interactions, while emitting per-turn state dynamics that track persuasion progress. We then introduce \textbf{Simulator-Induced Asymmetric-View Policy Optimization}, which converts the modeled concerns and the simulation state transition into complementary training objectives: (1) \emph{Asymmetric On-Policy Self-Distillation} that transfers concern-aware behavior from a privileged view of the same policy into its deployable, conversation-only view; and (2) \emph{State-Transition Policy Refinement} ...

</details>


### [222] [SciCore-Mol: Augmenting Large Language Models with Pluggable Molecular Cognition Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22287)
*Yuxuan Chen,Changwei Lv,Yunduo Xiao,Zhongjing Du,Daquan Zhou,Yukun Yan,Zheni Zeng,Zhiyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出SciCore-Mol框架，通过三个可插拔认知模块（拓扑感知、潜在扩散分子生成、反应推理）弥合大语言模型与异构科学数据（分子/反应）之间的语义鸿沟，实现8B参数开源系统在分子理解、生成、反应预测等任务上媲美甚至超越专有大型模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM处理分子等异构科学数据时，离散语言符号与拓扑分子/连续反应数据之间存在固有鸿沟，导致文本推理中的信息丢失和语义噪声。

Method: 提出SciCore-Mol模块化框架，包含三个可插拔认知模块：拓扑感知感知模块、基于潜在扩散的分子生成模块、反应感知推理模块。各模块通过学习的表征接口与LLM主干耦合，实现比纯文本工具反馈更丰富的信息交换。

Result: 在多种化学任务上，SciCore-Mol在分子理解、生成、反应预测及通用化学知识方面表现强劲，8B参数开源系统与专有大型模型竞争，部分维度超越之。

Conclusion: 该工作为LLM通过解耦、可插拔、灵活编排的模块获得科学专业能力提供了系统蓝图，对药物设计、化学合成及更广泛的科学发现具有直接意义。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are central to the one-for-all intelligent paradigm, but they face a fundamental challenge when dealing with heterogeneous scientific data such as molecules: the inherent gap between discrete linguistic symbols and topological molecular or continuous reaction data leads to significant information loss and semantic noise in text-based reasoning. We propose SciCore-Mol, a modular framework that bridges this gap through three deeply integrated pluggable cognitive modules: a topology-aware perception module, a latent diffusion-based molecular generation module, and a reaction-aware reasoning module. Each module is coupled to the LLM backbone through learned representation interfaces, enabling richer information exchange than is possible with text-only tool feedback. Our experiments on diverse chemical tasks demonstrate that SciCore-Mol achieves strong comprehensive performance across molecular understanding, generation, reaction prediction, and general chemistry knowledge, with an 8B-parameter open-source system that is competitive with and in several dimensions surpasses proprietary large models. This work provides a systematic blueprint for equipping LLMs with scientific expertise through decoupled, pluggable, and flexibly orchestrated modules, with direct implications for drug design, chemical synthesis, and broader scientific discovery.

</details>


### [223] [Cross-domain benchmarks reveal when coordinated AI agents improve scientific inference from partial evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22300)
*Fiona Y. Wong,Markus J. Buehler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出跨领域基准，评估多智能体AI协调在不同科学任务中是否优于简单基线。结果定义了三种操作区间：跨通道融合提升性能（如疾病检测AUROC 0.944、系外行星筛选AUROC 0.955）、信号主导时协调主要提升可解释性、分子声学任务中获得表征增益。协调价值仅在明确比较器支持下成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学证据常分散于多个仪器、数据库和学科，单一来源无法记录完整现象，因此难以判断协调式AI智能体何时比简单科学工作流更有价值。

Method: 构建跨域基准，涵盖四项科学任务：分子结构→音乐映射、科学范式转变检测、病媒传播疾病爆发识别、系外行星候选体筛选。采用固定评估面板、预定义评分协议、显式基线/消融/空对照、以及已知局限说明。

Result: 定义了三种操作区间：1) 跨通道融合提升性能（气候-媒介疾病AUROC 0.944，系外行星筛选AUROC 0.955）；2) 系外行星工作流与强摘要基线平手，分解未必提升性能；3) 信号主导时（范式转变）协调主要改善解释与可追溯性；分子声学增益为表征层面。

Conclusion: ScienceClaw x Infinite提供可审计工件与溯源层。基准表明，协调带来的性能、溯源或表征增益必须通过显式比较器验证，而非默认假设其价值。

Abstract: Scientific evidence often spans instruments, databases, and disciplines, so no single source records the full phenomenon. This makes it difficult to determine when coordinated AI agents add value over simpler scientific workflows. We evaluate this question with a cross-domain benchmark spanning four scientific tasks: mapping molecular structure into musical representations, detecting historical paradigm shifts in science, identifying vector-borne disease emergence, and vetting transiting-exoplanet candidates. Each case uses a frozen evaluation panel, predefined scoring protocols, explicit baselines, ablations or null controls, and stated limitations. The results define three operating regimes. When different disciplines each capture only part of the phenomenon, cross-channel composites improve over single-channel baselines: climate-vector emergence reaches AUROC 0.944 and exoplanet vetting reaches AUROC 0.955. However, the exoplanet workflow is effectively tied with a strong combined-summary baseline, showing that decomposition does not always improve top-line performance. When one signal dominates, as in paradigm-shift detection, coordination mainly improves interpretation and traceability. For molecular sonification, the gain is representational rather than predictive. ScienceClaw x Infinite provides the auditable artifact and provenance layer for this evaluation. The benchmark therefore assigns value to coordination only when the corresponding performance, provenance, or representation claim is supported by explicit comparators.

</details>


### [224] [Evaluation of Pipelines for Data Integration into Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22304)
*Marvin Hofer,Erhard Rahm*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出KGI-Bench基准，用于评估将新数据集成到已有知识图谱的不同管道的质量，通过覆盖率、正确性和一致性三个指标对12种管道进行对比评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏一种通用方法来评估知识图谱集成管道的整体质量和性能，无法确定最佳选择。

Method: 提出KGI-Bench基准，通过分析更新后KG的覆盖率、正确性和一致性三个互补质量指标来评估管道；提供电影领域的基准数据集（种子KG、三种格式的输入数据、参考KG作为真值）。

Result: 对12种不同管道进行了比较评估，分析了不同输入数据格式和设计选择对管道行为的影响。

Conclusion: KGI-Bench能够有效评估和比较不同知识图谱集成管道的质量，为选择最佳管道提供了系统化方法。

Abstract: Integrating new data into knowledge graphs (KG) typically involves different tasks that are executed within workflows or pipelines There are many possible pipelines for a specific integration problem but there is not yet a general approach to evaluate the overall quality and performance of such pipelines to be able to determine the best choices. We therefore propose a new benchmark KGI-Bench to evaluate integration pipelines that ingest different kinds of input data into an existing KG. We evaluate pipelines by analyzing their output, i.e., the updated KG, with the three complementary quality metrics coverage, correctness and consistency. We also provide benchmark datasets (seed KG, overlapping input data of three formats, reference KG as a ground truth) for the movie domain. To demonstrate the applicability and usefulness of the proposed benchmark, we comparatively evaluate 12 pipelines and analyze their behavior across different input data formats and design choices.

</details>


### [225] [Meta-Soft: Leveraging Composable Meta-Tokens for Context-Preserving KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22337)
*Wei Luo,Yi Huang,Songchen Ma,Huanyu Qu,Jiang Cai,Mingkun Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Meta-Soft动态压缩框架，通过可学习的正交基矩阵和Gumbel-Softmax选择网络动态合成Soft Tokens，并引入注意力流整合机制将丢弃令牌的语义信息重分配到保留令牌中，解决KV Cache压缩中静态参数无法适应动态输入以及永久丢弃导致信息丢失的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于固定Soft Tokens的KV Cache驱逐方法（如Judge Q）依赖静态参数集来评估KV对重要性，无法动态适应不同输入提示，也无法精确捕捉复杂多变的任务相关性；且被驱逐的KV对被永久丢弃，导致不可逆的信息丢失和上下文断裂。

Method: 1) 构建包含可学习正交基矩阵L的元库；2) 使用带Gumbel-Softmax的选择网络产生可微分的稀疏组合权重，从输入提示特征中动态合成最相关的k个Soft Tokens；3) 将这些Soft Tokens附加到输入序列末尾来探测关键信息；4) 引入基于注意力流的整合机制，将移除令牌的语义信息重新分配到保留令牌中。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，该方法优于现有最先进的驱逐方法，为KV Cache压缩提供了新的解决方案。

Conclusion: Meta-Soft通过动态合成Soft Tokens和注意力流整合机制，有效解决了KV Cache压缩中的动态适配和信息保留问题，显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: The KV cache used in large language models has linearly growing time complexity, so LLMs face memory blow-up and reduced decoding efficiency when they process long contexts.Current KV Cache eviction has become an important research direction; however, existing methods based on fixed Soft Tokens (e.g., Judge Q) rely on a static parameter set as the query to evaluate the importance of KV pairs, so they cannot adapt dynamically to different input prompts, and they cannot precisely capture complex and changing task relevance.Also, evicted KV pairs are discarded permanently, so this causes irreversible information loss and context breaks. To address this problem, we propose Meta-Soft, a dynamic compression framework based on probe-driven context integration. Specifically, we build a meta-library with a learnable orthogonal basis matrix $\mathcal{L}$, and we use a selector network with Gumbel-Softmax to produce differentiable sparse combination weights, so we dynamically synthesize the most targeted $k$ Soft Tokens from the input prompt features.We append these Soft Tokens to the end of the input sequence to probe key information. We also introduce an attention-flow based integration mechanism, which redistributes the semantic information of removed tokens into retained tokens, and this keeps the dropped context information effectively.Experiments on multiple datasets show that our method outperforms existing state-of-the-art eviction methods and provides a new solution for KV Cache compression.

</details>


### [226] [Scaling Observation-aware Planning in Uncertain Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22364)
*Adrian Zvizdenco,Arthur Conrado Veiga Bosquetti,Alberto Lluch Lafuente,Christoph Matheja*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究在不确定环境中为智能体选择传感能力的问题，提出基于POMDP分解的新方法，将传感器选择问题和位置可观测性问题的求解性能提升3-5个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不确定领域中，如何在任务可实现性与硬件/处理的高成本之间平衡传感能力的选择是一个基础工程挑战。

Method: 除了改进基于参数合成的原始方法，还开发了一种新求解方法，通过分解POMDP来识别合理的观测函数。

Result: 新方法在实例规模和运行时间上分别提升了3个和5个数量级的性能。

Conclusion: 通过POMDP分解的方式可以有效扩展可判定片段的求解规模，大幅提升最优可观测性问题的求解效率。

Abstract: Deciding which sensing capabilities to deploy on an agent in uncertain domains is a fundamental engineering challenge, in which one balances task achievability against the high costs of hardware and processing. This problem has previously been formalized as the Optimal Observability Problem (OOP), based on the well-known Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (POMDP) model for decision-making. This work studies (sub-)symbolic techniques to scale solving of decidable fragments of the OOP, namely the Sensor Selection Problem (SSP) and the Positional Observability Problem (POP). Besides improving the original approach based on parameter synthesis, we develop a new solving method that identifies sensible observation functions via decomposition of POMDPs, improving performance by 3 and 5 orders of magnitude for instance size and runtime, respectively.

</details>


### [227] [Epicure: Navigating the Emergent Geometry of Food Ingredient Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22391)
*Jakub Radzikowski,Josef Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Epicure 是三个基于多语言菜谱语料库（414万条菜谱，覆盖9种语言）从头训练的skip-gram食材嵌入模型，通过LLM增强管线标准化为1790个规范食材条目，并基于共现图与风味化合物图构建了三个Metapath2Vec变体（Cooc纯共现、Chem纯化合物、Core混合），分别位于化学-菜谱上下文光谱的不同位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有食材嵌入大多基于单一语言或仅依赖共现信息，缺乏对食材化学特性的系统整合。作者希望构建一个既能反映菜谱上下文又能捕捉食材风味化学成分的多语言食材嵌入模型。

Method: 收集414万条多语言菜谱，经LLM辅助管道标准化为1790个规范食材。构建共现图（NPMI）和FlavorDB化合物图，基于Metapath2Vec训练三个变体：Cooc（纯共现游走）、Chem（纯化合物元路径游走）、Core（混合游走），三者架构和超参数相同，仅随机游走策略不同。

Result: 生成了三个位于化学-菜谱光谱不同位置的食材嵌入模型，以及203,508条边的食材共现图和80,019条边的食材-化合物类型图（含2,247个化合物节点、15个类型）。

Conclusion: Epicure通过多语言菜谱数据和化学-共现混合游走策略，提供了一套可调节化学知识与菜谱上下文平衡的食材嵌入家族，为后续食材替换、风味预测等任务提供了基础资源。

Abstract: We present Epicure, a family of three sibling skip-gram ingredient embeddings retrained from scratch on a multilingual recipe corpus. We aggregate 4.14M recipes from 11 sources spanning seven languages, English, Chinese, Russian, Vietnamese, Spanish, Turkish, Indonesian, German, and Indian-English, and normalise the raw ingredient strings to 1,790 canonical entries via an LLM-augmented pipeline. A 203,508-edge ingredient-ingredient NPMI graph and an 80,019-edge typed FlavorDB ingredient-compound graph, 2,247 typed compound nodes across 15 categories, seed three Metapath2Vec variants that share architecture and hyperparameters and differ only in the random-walk schema: Cooc walks the co-occurrence graph only, Chem walks the typed compound metapaths only, and Core blends both via injected ingredient-ingredient walks at controlled mixing, placing each model at a distinct point on the chemistry-vs-recipe-context spectrum.

</details>


### [228] [S2ED: From Story to Executable Descriptions for Consistency-Aware Story Illustration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22448)
*Sijing Yin,Jiamou Liu,Xiao Tang,Yaser Shakib,Qian Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出S2ED，一个无需训练、模型无关的提示层框架，通过将故事转化为可执行描述序列，利用三个智能体协同工作（叙事分割、角色属性锚定、空间与情感线索丰富），显著提升多帧故事插画的长程一致性、角色保真度，并在Flintstones和Shakoo Maku数据集上超越强基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多帧故事插画需要跨帧的长程一致性（叙事分解、角色身份、布局、情感），而单图像文本生成模型无法直接处理这种跨帧连贯性问题，现有方法在保持角色一致性和叙事连贯性方面存在不足。

Method: S2ED（Story-to-Executable Descriptions）框架，是一种训练免费、模型无关的提示层方法。核心机制包括：1) 三个智能体协同——叙事分割智能体、角色属性锚定智能体、空间与情感线索丰富智能体；2) 可解释的提示承载状态传播；3) 局部编辑修复漂移，无需重训练生成器。

Result: 在Flintstones和Shakoo Maku数据集上，S2ED在序列级一致性和角色保真度方面，超越了强提示方法、大模型规划方法以及基于参考训练的方法，在自动指标和人工评判下均表现更优。

Conclusion: S2ED提供了一种有效且灵活的解决方案来提升多帧故事插画的连贯性，已部署到端到端的故事到故事书系统中，展示了实际应用价值。

Abstract: Multi-frame story illustration requires long-horizon coherence beyond single-image text-to-image generation, including narrative decomposition and persistent character identity, layout, and affect across frames. We propose Story-to-Executable Descriptions (S2ED), a training-free, model-agnostic, prompt-layer framework that converts a full story into a sequence of explicit, editable executable descriptions for more consistent rendering. S2ED coordinates three agents to segment the narrative, ground canonical character attributes, and enrich spatial and affective cues, enabling interpretable prompt-carried state propagation and local edits to repair drift without retraining the generator. Experiments on Flintstones and Shakoo Maku show that S2ED improves sequence-level consistency and character fidelity over strong prompting, large-model planning, and a reference training-based method, under both automatic metrics and human judgments. We also deploy S2ED in an end-to-end story-to-storybook system for children's illustrated stories, with a supplementary video.

</details>


### [229] [KAPPS: A knowledge-based CPPS Architecture for the Circular Factory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22457)
*Etienne Hoffmann,Jan-Felix Klein,Sören Weindel,Max Goebels,Sebastian Behrendt,Daniel Hernández,Ratan Bahadur Thapa,Jürgen Fleischer,Kai Furmans,Steffen Staab*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对循环制造中产品状态异质、过程动态可变的需求，提出基于知识图谱的KAPPS架构，通过本体驱动的知识图作为统一数据骨干与语义接口层，实现异构系统集成、推理与实时约束执行，并在异常检测和模块化传送带系统中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统线性制造依赖同质材料和预定流程，而循环制造需要处理使用后产品的不确定和异质状态。现有制造IT架构无法表示和管理运行时单个组件的独特性，亟需新架构。

Method: 采用设计科学方法论，从五个互补视角推导14条需求；设计KAPPS架构：本体驱动的知识图谱作为数据骨干，语义接口层实现异构系统集成，包含约束执行与事件驱动规划模块。

Result: 通过两个用例验证：1) 基于知识图谱服务的异常检测与学习；2) 模块化传送带系统中的运行时约束执行。并对14条需求进行架构评估。

Conclusion: KAPPS能将知识图谱从集成层转变为工厂的权威写入时状态，支持增量式执行计划调整和人机知识交换，适用于循环制造场景。

Abstract: While linear manufacturing relies on homogeneous materials and predefined process sequences, circular manufacturing reintroduces used products with heterogeneous and uncertain conditions. This shift demands manufacturing systems capable of handling variable product states, dynamically reconfigurable processes, and the integration of human and machine knowledge. Conventional manufacturing IT architectures, designed for stable structures and deterministic execution, are unable to meet these requirements, as they cannot adequately represent and manage the uniqueness of individual components at runtime. Following a design science methodology for developing a Cyber Physical Production System for circular manufacturing, we derive 14 requirements from five complementary perspectives. Based on these requirements, we design KAPPS, a knowledge-based architecture that uses an ontology-grounded knowledge graph as a unifying data backbone, combined with a semantic interface layer to enable consistent data and information integration, reasoning, and communication across heterogeneous systems and services, turning the knowledge graph from an integration layer into the factories authoritative write-time state. KAPPS incorporates modules for constraint enforcement and event-driven planning, enabling incremental adaptation of execution plans under uncertainty and human-machine knowledge exchange. The applicability of KAPPS is demonstrated through two implemented use cases: (i) Anomaly detection and learning through knowledge graph mediated services and (ii) runtime constraint enforcement in a modular conveyor system. Subsequently, the architecture is evaluated against the 14 requirements (ed. abstract shortened)

</details>


### [230] [Compiling Agentic Workflows into LLM Weights: Near-Frontier Quality at Two Orders of Magnitude Less Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22502)
*Simon Dennis,Rivaan Patil,Kevin Shabahang,Hao Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现AI agent编排框架使用外部编排器存在上下文消耗、需前沿模型、暴露专有流程等问题，提出通过将流程编译到小型微调模型权重中（"地下agent"）来替代，并针对三个感知障碍在三个领域进行了实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有agent编排框架（如LangGraph、CrewAI等）采用外部编排器模式，存在上下文窗口消耗、必须使用前沿模型、且暴露专有流程给第三方提供商等问题，亟需更高效、更安全的替代方案。

Method: 提出"地下agent"方法：将流程编译到小型微调模型的权重中，而非在运行时通过外部编排器注入指令。针对开发者的三个感知障碍进行实证验证，涵盖旅行预订（14个节点）、Zoom支持（14个节点+产品知识）和保险理赔（55个节点+6个决策枢纽）三个领域。

Result: 在三个实际领域（旅行预订、Zoom支持、保险理赔）中验证了该方法的可行性，表明地下agent可以有效替代外部编排器方案。

Conclusion: 尽管开发者目前偏向采用外部编排器方案，但将流程编译到小型微调模型中的"地下agent"方法能解决编排器模式的三大问题，开发者主要存在三个感知障碍，本文通过实证研究逐一回应了这些障碍。

Abstract: Agent orchestration frameworks have proliferated, collectively exceeding 290,000 GitHub stars across LangGraph, CrewAI, Google ADK, OpenAI Agents SDK, Semantic Kernel, Strands, and LlamaIndex. All follow the same pattern: an external orchestrator above the LLM, injecting instructions and routing decisions every turn. Recent work has shown this architecture is dominated for procedural tasks by simply providing the procedure in a frontier model's system prompt [Dennis et al., 2026a], at the cost of consuming the context window, requiring a frontier model for every conversation, and exposing proprietary procedures to third-party providers. Compiling the procedure into the weights of a small fine-tuned model -- creating a subterranean agent -- should resolve all of these concerns, and prior work (SimpleTOD, FireAct, SynTOD, WorkflowLLM, Agent Lumos) has shown the technique works. Yet developer adoption has overwhelmingly favored orchestration. We identify three perceived barriers and address each empirically across travel booking (14 nodes), Zoom support (14 nodes, product-specific knowledge), and insurance claims (55 nodes, 6 decision hubs).

</details>


### [231] [LACO: Adaptive Latent Communication for Collaborative Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22504)
*Tianhao Chen,Yuheng Wu,Dongman Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LACO，一种无需额外训练的潜在通信范式，通过迭代潜在推理、跨视野显著性归因和结构化语义知识蒸馏，在降低延迟的同时保持协作驾驶性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言通信在协作驾驶中存在高延迟和信息丢失问题，而现有潜在通信在多智能体环境下存在智能体身份混淆。

Method: 提出LACO，包含三个组件：迭代潜在推理(ILD)进行潜在空间推理、跨视野显著性归因(CHSA)选择通信高效信息、结构化语义知识蒸馏(SSKD)稳定自我中心决策。

Result: 在CARLA闭环实验中，LACO显著降低了通信和推理延迟，同时保持了强大的协作驾驶性能。

Conclusion: 潜在通信范式LACO有效克服了语言通信的延迟问题与直接潜在融合的身份混淆问题，为协作驾驶提供了一种高效、即插即用的解决方案。

Abstract: Collaborative driving aims to improve safety and efficiency by enabling connected vehicles to coordinate under partial observability. Recent approaches have evolved from sharing visual features for perception to exchanging language-based reasoning through foundation models for behavioral coordination. Though communicating in language provides intuitive information, it introduces two challenges: high latency caused by autoregressive decoding and information loss caused by compressing rich internal representations into discrete tokens. To address these challenges, we analyze latent communication in collaborative driving under inherent limitations of multi-agent settings. Our analysis reveals agent identity confusion, where direct fusion of latent states entangles decision representations across vehicles. Motivated by this, we propose LACO, a training-free \textbf{LA}tent \textbf{CO}mmunication paradigm that seamlessly adapts pretrained driving models to collaborative settings. LACO introduces Iterative Latent Deliberation (ILD) for latent reasoning, Cross-Horizon Saliency Attribution (CHSA) for communication-efficient information selection, and Structured Semantic Knowledge Distillation (SSKD) to stabilize ego-centric decision making. Closed-loop experiments in CARLA show that LACO notably reduces communication and inference latency while maintaining strong collaborative driving performance.

</details>


### [232] [Towards Direct Evaluation of Harness Optimizers via Priority Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22505)
*Kai Tzu-iunn Ong,Minseok Kang,Dongwook Choi,Junhee Cho,Seungju Kim,Seungwon Lim,Geunha Jang,Minwoo Oh,Bogyung Jeong,Sunghwan Kim,Taeyoon Kwon,Jinyoung Yeo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出优先级排序（priority ranking）方法，通过让优化器对harness组件进行排序来直接评估其能力，无需昂贵展开。基于182个场景的Shor数据集验证了该方法与真实优化能力的相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有harness优化研究仅通过目标agent的性能提升间接评估优化器，忽略了中间步骤的错误动作，无法区分优化器是靠真正推理还是试错来工作，缺乏直接评估方法。

Method: 提出优先级排序（priority ranking）方法，让优化器对harness中各组件按更新潜力进行排序，量化其步骤级能力；并构建包含182个人工验证优化场景的Shor数据集。

Result: 优化器的排序能力与其在多步骤实际harness优化中的表现显著相关，验证了优先级排序可作为优化能力的可靠预测指标。

Conclusion: 优先级排序是一种简单、低成本且有效的直接评估harness优化器的方法，能够可靠预测优化器的实际优化能力。

Abstract: Harness optimization enables automated agent creation by having an optimizer agent iteratively update the harness of target agents. Despite its success, current studies evaluate optimizers solely by observing target agents' performance gains. This indirect end-improvement evaluation neglects optimizers' actions at intermediate steps, which are often erroneous and hinder agent performance. Therefore, it is unclear whether harness optimization is driven by optimizers' informed update actions or simply trial-and-error. This necessitates direct evaluation of harness optimizers. However, evaluating harness optimizers directly is non-trivial and costly due to the lack of oracle harnesses. To address this, we present a simple, low-cost design to directly evaluate them, namely priority ranking. By asking harness optimizers to rank components (e.g., tools) in a given harness by their potential to improve/hinder agent performance when updated, our design quantifies optimizer ability at the step level without expensive rollouts or manual examination. More importantly, optimizers' ranking performance correlates with their ability to improve agents in actual multi-step harness optimization, establishing priority ranking as a reliable predictor of optimization ability. Priority ranking is enabled by Shor, a collection of 182 human-verified optimization scenarios spanning across domains, designs, and time stages. Codes and data can be found at https://github.com/k59118/Harness_Optimizer_Evaluation.

</details>


### [233] [Search-E1: Self-Distillation Drives Self-Evolution in Search-Augmented Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22511)
*Zihan Liang,Yufei Ma,Ben Chen,Zhipeng Qian,Xuxin Zhang,Huangyu Dai,Lingtao Mao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Search-E1，一种仅通过GRPO+离线自蒸馏即可提升搜索增强推理代理性能的自我进化方法，无需外部监督、辅助模块或复杂奖励设计，在7个QA基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前后训练搜索增强代理的方法越来越复杂（引入外部监督、奖励模型、树搜索等），作者质疑这些复杂机制是否真正必要，希望探索更简洁的方案。

Method: 采用vanilla GRPO与离线自蒸馏（OFSD）交织的自我进化方法：每轮GRPO后，模型在自己训练问题上rollout，通过token级前向KL散度让模型推理分布对齐其在特权上下文（暴露更高效兄弟轨迹）下的自身分布，从而提供密集逐步监督。

Result: 在7个QA基准上，Search-E1（Qwen2.5-3B）达到0.440平均EM，超越所有开源基线。

Conclusion: 复杂的外部机制并非后训练搜索增强代理的必要条件，简单的自进化方法即可达到甚至超越复杂方案的性能。

Abstract: Post-training has become the dominant recipe for turning a language model into a competent search-augmented reasoning agent. A line of recent work pushes its performance further by adding elaborate machinery on top of this standard pipeline. These augmentations import external supervision from stronger external systems, attach auxiliary modules such as process reward models or retrospective critics, restructure the rollout itself with tree search or multi-stage curricula, or shape the reward with hand-crafted bonuses and penalties. Each addition delivers a measurable gain, but each also inflates the training pipeline and ties the recipe to resources or designs that may not always be available. We take a step back and ask whether any of this machinery is actually necessary, and propose Search-E1, a self-evolution method that lets a search-augmented agent improve through only vanilla GRPO interleaved with offline self-distillation (OFSD). After each GRPO round, the policy rolls out on its own training questions. A token-level forward KL objective then aligns the policy's inference-time distribution to its own distribution under a privileged context that exposes a more efficient sibling trajectory. Despite this simplicity, the procedure naturally provides dense per-step supervision. On seven QA benchmarks, Search-E1 reaches $0.440$ average EM with Qwen2.5-3B, surpassing all open-source baselines at both scales. Code and complete version will be made public soon.

</details>


### [234] [Meta-Learning for Rapid Adaptation in Reference Tracking of Uncertain Nonlinear Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22513)
*Jiaqi Yan,Ankush Chakrabarty,Niklas Schmid,John Lygeros,Alisa Rupenyan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于元学习的控制框架，通过结合iMAML算法，利用源系统数据离线训练共享表示，再在目标系统上仅用少量数据进行在线微调，解决了不确定非线性系统在有限数据下的参考跟踪控制问题。该框架泛化性强，支持神经状态空间模型和深度Q网络两种算法，仿真和硬件实验均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标系统数据难以获取，现有方法无法在有限数据下实现最优控制。元学习范式允许利用源系统的离线数据加速训练并提升控制性能。

Method: 提出了基于iMAML的元学习控制框架，包含离线元训练（从源系统学习共享动力学表示）和在线元适应（在目标系统上少量样本微调）。将问题建模为双层优化，降低了存储复杂度。框架内集成了神经状态空间模型和深度Q网络两种算法。

Result: 数值仿真和硬件实验表明，所提方法在控制性能上持续优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 基于元学习的框架能够有效利用源系统数据，在目标系统数据有限的条件下实现更优的控制性能，且具有较好的泛化性和灵活性。

Abstract: In this paper, we address the problem of reference tracking for uncertain nonlinear systems. Since collecting data from the target system (i.e., the system of interest) is often challenging, our objective is to design optimal controllers using limited target system data. Meta-learning provides a promising paradigm by leveraging offline data from source systems (systems sharing structural similarities with the target system) to accelerate training and enhance control performance. Motivated by this idea, we propose a meta-learning-based control framework that tailors the implicit model-agnostic meta-learning (iMAML) algorithm to the control setting. The framework operates in two phases: an (offline) meta-training phase, where an aggregated representation is learned from source data to capture the shared system dynamics among similar systems, and an (online) meta-adaptation phase, where this representation is fine-tuned on the target system using only a few data samples and limited adaptation steps. We formulate this framework as a bi-level optimization problem and provide an efficient solution with reduced storage complexity and few approximations. The proposed framework is general, allowing various learning algorithms to be integrated. To demonstrate this flexibility, we propose two specific learning algorithms that can be incorporated into our framework based on a neural state-space model and a deep Q-network, respectively. The primary distinction between these approaches is whether explicit system identification is required. Numerical simulations and hardware experiments demonstrate that the proposed methods enhance control performance and consistently outperform baseline approaches.

</details>


### [235] [A Subjective Logic-based method for runtime confidence updates in safety arguments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22530)
*Benjamin Herd,Jessica Kelly,Clarissa Heinemann,João-Vitor Zacchi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种动态定量保证方法，通过运行时安全性能指标（SPI）持续更新静态安全案例中的置信度，使用主观逻辑（SL）统一设计时证据与运行时数据，并基于惩罚优先的更新规则实现安全响应的即时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统静态安全案例无法反映系统运行时状态变化，缺乏对置信度的动态量化与更新机制，难以应对ML组件等不确定性较高的系统安全保障需求。

Method: 构建基于主观逻辑（SL）的统一保证案例，融合设计时证据与滑动窗口SPI；运行时持续评估SPI证据，采用非贝叶斯的惩罚优先更新规则：无违规时提升置信度，出现违规时立即施加惩罚。

Result: 通过模拟的施工区辅助功能（含ML锥桶检测）演示，展示了运行时SPI观测下置信度的动态演化过程。

Conclusion: 该方法能在运行时优先响应安全相关的置信度变化，比经典的贝叶斯后验更新更适合安全关键系统的动态保证需求。

Abstract: We present a method for dynamic quantitative assurance that enhances static safety cases with continuous, runtime-driven confidence updates. The method quantifies and propagates confidence across the development lifecycle by integrating design-time evidence and windowed runtime Safety Performance Indicators (SPIs) within a single Subjective Logic (SL)-based assurance case. At runtime, SPI evidence is continuously evaluated, and targeted claims are updated using a rule that increases confidence in the absence of violations and imposes prompt penalties when violations occur. This design prioritizes safety-relevant responsiveness over exact classical Bayesian posterior updates. We demonstrate the method using a simulation-based construction zone assist function, focusing on an ML-based construction cone detection component, and show how confidence evolves as SPI evidence is observed in operation.

</details>


### [236] [TerminalWorld: Benchmarking Agents on Real-World Terminal Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22535)
*Zhaoyang Chu,Jiarui Hu,Xingyu Jiang,Pengyu Zou,Han Li,Chao Peng,Peter O'Hearn,Earl T. Barr,Mark Harman,Federica Sarro,He Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TerminalWorld是一个可扩展的数据引擎，能从真实终端录​​音中自动逆向工程出高保真评估任务。基于80,870条终端录音，生成了包含1,530个验证任务的基准，覆盖18个真实世界类别。对前沿模型和智能体的基准测试显示，最佳通过率仅62.5%，且与现有专家策划基准的相关性很弱（r=0.20）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有终端智能体评估基准多由专家手工策划，缺乏真实性和可扩展性，难以反映开发者实际使用终端环境的真实能力。作者希望构建一个自动、可扩展的评估框架，直接从真实终端录​​音中提取任务，以更真实地衡量智能体在终端环境中的表现。

Method: 提出TerminalWorld数据引擎，自动从“野外”终端录​​音中逆向工程出评估任务。处理80,870条终端录音，生成1,530个验证任务（覆盖18个类别、1,280个独特命令），并从中精选200个代表性任务形成Verified子集。在多种前沿模型和六种智能体上进行综合基准测试。

Result: 最佳模型通过率仅62.5%。TerminalWorld与现有专家基准（如Terminal-Bench）的评分相关性很弱（Pearson r=0.20），表明其捕捉了不同的真实终端能力。

Conclusion: TerminalWorld通过自动化、可扩展的构建方式，提供了更真实、更具代表性的终端智能体评估基准，能随开发者实践演变而持续更新，揭示了现有智能体在真实终端任务上的显著不足。

Abstract: We introduce TerminalWorld, a scalable data engine that automatically reverse-engineers high-fidelity evaluation tasks from "in-the-wild" terminal recordings. Processing 80,870 terminal recordings, the engine yields a full benchmark of 1,530 validated tasks, spanning 18 real-world categories, ranging from short everyday operations to workflows exceeding 50 steps, and covering 1,280 unique commands. From these, we curate a Verified subset of 200 representative, manually reviewed tasks. Comprehensive benchmarking on TerminalWorld-Verified across eight frontier models and six agents reveals that current systems still struggle with authentic terminal workflows, achieving a maximum pass rate of only 62.5%. Moreover, TerminalWorld captures real-world terminal capabilities distinct from existing expert-curated benchmarks (e.g., Terminal-Bench), with only a weak correlation to their scores (Pearson r=0.20). The automated engine makes TerminalWorld authentic and scalable by construction, enabling it to evaluate agents in real-world terminal environments as developer practices evolve. Data and code are available at https://github.com/EuniAI/TerminalWorld.

</details>


### [237] [Think Thrice Before You Speak: Dual knowledge-enhanced Theory-of-Mind Reasoning for Persuasive Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22602)
*Minghui Ma,Bin Guo,Runze Yang,Mengqi Chen,Yan Liu,Jingqi Liu,Yahan Pei,Xuehao Ma,Qiuyun Zhang,Zhiwen Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于心理理论的说服对话任务(ToM-PD)，构建大规模标注数据集ToM-BPD，并提出知识增强的分步推理框架TTBYS，实验表明Qwen3-8B+TTBYS在预测欲望、信念和说服策略上分别超越GPT-5达1.20%、22.80%和16.97%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM由于依赖简单提示策略和缺乏心理理论(ToM)知识，难以捕捉心理状态间的内在依赖关系，导致表征碎片化和推理不稳定。

Method: 1) 提出ToM-PD任务，基于BDI框架显式建模多轮对话中心理状态的序列依赖关系；2) 构建大规模标注数据集ToM-BPD；3) 提出TTBYS框架，利用显式和隐式先验经验增强LLM对欲望、信念和说服策略的推理能力。

Result: Qwen3-8B+TTBYS在欲望预测、信念预测和说服策略预测上分别超过GPT-5达1.20%、22.80%和16.97%。

Conclusion: TTBYS方法增强了推理的可解释性和一致性，有效解决了LLM在说服对话中ToM推理的不足。

Abstract: Persuasive dialogue requires reasoning about others' latent mental states, a capability known as Theory of Mind (ToM). However, due to reliance on simple prompting strategies and insufficient ToM knowledge, existing LLMs often fail to capture the intrinsic dependencies among mental states, leading to fragmented representations and unstable reasoning. To address these challenges, we introduce the ToM-based Persuasive Dialogue (ToM-PD) task, grounded in the Belief-Desire-Intention (BDI) framework, which explicitly models the sequential dependencies among mental states in multi-turn dialogues. To facilitate research on this task, we construct a large-scale annotated dataset, ToM-based Broad Persuasive Dialogues (ToM-BPD), capturing fine-grained mental states and corresponding persuasive strategies. We further propose Think Thrice Before You Speak (TTBYS), a knowledge-enhanced stepwise reasoning framework that leverages both explicit and implicit prior experiences to improve LLMs' inference of desires, beliefs, and persuasive strategies. Experimental results demonstrate that Qwen3-8B equipped with TTBYS outperforms GPT-5 by 1.20%, 22.80%, and 16.97% in predicting desires, beliefs, and persuasive strategies, respectively. Case studies further show that our approach enhances interpretability and consistency in reasoning.

</details>


### [238] [Spreadsheet-RL: Advancing Large Language Model Agents on Realistic Spreadsheet Tasks via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22642)
*Banghao Chi,Yining Xie,Mingyuan Wu,Jingcheng Yang,Jize Jiang,Zhaoheng Li,Shengyi Qian,Minjia Zhang,Klara Nahrstedt,Rui Hou,Xiangjun Fan,Hanchao Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Spreadsheet-RL，一个基于强化学习的微调框架，用于训练专门的电子表格智能体。该框架包含自动化数据收集流水线、领域特定评估基准（Domain-Spreadsheet）以及一个支持多轮强化学习的Spreadsheet Gym环境。实验表明，该方法在通用和领域特定电子表格任务上均显著提升了性能（如Qwen3-4B-Think在SpreadsheetBench上的Pass@1从12.0%提升至23.4%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电子表格智能体依赖通用大语言模型的特殊提示设计，在简单操作上有效，但在处理真实世界复杂的多步骤工作流程时表现不佳。因此需要专门训练电子表格智能体的框架。

Method: 1) 自动化流水线：从在线论坛大规模收集成对的起始-目标电子表格数据；2) Domain-Spreadsheet基准数据集：涵盖金融、供应链管理等领域的评估任务；3) Spreadsheet Gym环境：通过Python沙箱暴露Excel功能，配备完善的工具集和工具路由规则，支持多轮强化学习。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B-Think模型上，Spreadsheet-Bench的Pass@1从12.0%提升至23.4%；Domain-Spreadsheet数据集的Pass@1从8.4%提升至17.2%。

Conclusion: Spreadsheet-RL显著增强了AI智能体在通用和领域特定电子表格任务上的性能，展现出良好的泛化能力和实际应用潜力，为推进基于LLM的数据界面交互提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Spreadsheet systems (e.g., Microsoft Excel, Google Sheets) play a central role in modern data-centric workflows. As AI agents grow increasingly capable of automating complex tasks, such as controlling computers and generating presentations, building an AI-driven spreadsheet agent has emerged as a promising research direction. Most existing spreadsheet agents rely on specialized prompting over general-purpose LLMs; while this design has potentials on simple spreadsheet operations, it struggles to manage the complex, multi-step workflows typical of real-world applications.
  We introduce Spreadsheet-RL, a reinforcement learning (RL) fine-tuning framework designed to train specialized spreadsheet agents within a realistic Microsoft Excel environment. Spreadsheet-RL features an automated pipeline for scalable collection of paired start-goal spreadsheets from online forums, as well as domain-specific evaluation tasks in areas such as finance and supply chain management, which we compile into the new Domain-Spreadsheet benchmark dataset. It also includes a Spreadsheet Gym environment designed for multi-turn RL: Spreadsheet Gym exposes extensive Excel functionality through a Python sandbox, along with a refined harness that incorporates a comprehensive tool set and carefully designed tool-routing rules for spreadsheet tasks. Through comprehensive experiments, we show that Spreadsheet-RL substantially enhances AI agent's performance on both general and domain-specific spreadsheet tasks: it improves Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507's Pass@1 on SpreadsheetBench from 12.0% to 23.4%, and raises Pass@1 from 8.4% to 17.2% on our curated Domain-Spreadsheet dataset. These results highlight Spreadsheet-RL's strong potential for generalization and real-world adoption in spreadsheet automation, and broadly, its promise for advancing LLM-based interactions with data interfaces in everyday work.

</details>


### [239] [AtelierEval: Agentic Evaluation of Humans & LLMs as Text-to-Image Prompters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22645)
*Hanjun Luo,Zhimu Huang,Sylvia Chung,Yiran Wang,Yingbin Jin,Jialin Li,Jiang Li,Xinfeng Li,Hanan Salam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了AtelierEval，首个统一基准，用于衡量文本到图像系统中上游提示器（人类或MLLM）的提示能力，涵盖360个专家设计的任务，并引入AtelierJudge评估器，实现接近人类水平的评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的T2I基准仅评估下游模型，忽略了上游提示器将用户意图转化为详细提示的能力，这一关键环节未被量化。

Method: 构建包含360个专家任务的AtelierEval基准（基于认知视角分类），提出AtelierJudge评估器（基于技能、记忆增强的代理式评估器），输出主观和客观评分。

Result: AtelierJudge与人类专家的Spearman相关系数为0.79，接近人类水平；实验发现模仿优于规划，建议未来提示器向图像增强方向发展。

Conclusion: AtelierEval可作为鲁棒的诊断工具，揭示了当前提示器的局限性，并为未来提示器设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) systems increasingly rely on upstream prompters, either humans or multimodal large language models (MLLMs), to translate user intent into detailed prompts. Yet current benchmarks fix the prompt and only evaluate T2I models, leaving the prompting proficiency of this upstream component entirely unmeasured. We introduce AtelierEval, the first unified benchmark that quantifies prompting proficiency across 360 expert-crafted tasks. Grounded in a cognitive view, it spans three task categories and instantiates tasks using a taxonomy of real-world challenges, with a dual interface for both humans and MLLMs. To enable scalable and reliable evaluation, we propose AtelierJudge, a skill-based, memory-augmented agentic evaluator. It produces subjective and objective scores for prompt-image pairs, achieving a Spearman correlation of 0.79 with human experts, approaching human performance. Extensive experiments benchmark 8 MLLMs against 48 human users across 4 T2I backends, validate AtelierEval as a robust diagnostic tool, and reveal the superiority of mimicry over planning, advocating for an image-augmented direction for future prompters. Our work is released to support future research.

</details>


### [240] [Claw AI Lab: An Autonomous Multi-Agent Research Team](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22662)
*Fan Wu,Cheng Chen,Zhenshan Tan,Taiyu Zhang,Xinzhen Xu,Yanyu Qian,Dingcheng Gao,Lanyun Zhu,Qi Zhu,Yi Tan,Deyi Ji,Guosheng Lin,Tianrun Chen,Deheng Ye,Fayao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Claw AI Lab 是一个实验室原生自主研究平台，将自动化研究从隐藏的提示到论文流程转变为交互式 AI 实验室。它支持用户通过单一提示实例化完整研究团队，提供可自定义角色、协作工作流、实时监控、工件检查和回滚/恢复控制，并通过 Claw-Code Harness 连接本地代码、数据集和检查点，提升实验可重复性和结果完整性。内部评估显示其在创意新颖性、实验完整性和论文展示质量上优于基线系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化研究平台多为中心化单智能体或固定串行流水线，缺乏交互性、可操控性和实验室般的实际使用体验，导致实验难以检查、迭代和忠实转化为最终论文。

Method: Claw AI Lab 采用多智能体团队架构，支持用户通过提示实例化完整研究团队；提供统一仪表盘实现实时监控、工件检查和回滚/恢复控制；包含 Claw-Code Harness 连接本地代码库、数据集和检查点以支持可运行实验，并将执行工件反馈回研究循环。

Result: 在五个 AI 研究案例的内部评估中，以 AutoResearchClaw 为基线，AI 专家评审在创意新颖性、实验完整性和论文展示质量上均一致更偏好 Claw AI Lab。

Conclusion: Claw AI Lab 是向自主研究作为可用、交互式且可靠性感知的科学基础设施这一新范式的早期探索。

Abstract: We present Claw AI Lab, a lab-native autonomous research platform that advances automated research from a hidden prompt-to-paper pipeline into an interactive AI laboratory. Rather than centering the system around a single agent or a fixed serial workflow, we allow users to instantiate a full research team from one prompt, with customizable roles, collaborative workflows, real-time monitoring, artifact inspection, and rollback/resume control through a unified dashboard. The platform also supports distinct research modes for exploration, multi-agent discussion, and reproduction, making autonomous research substantially more steerable and laboratory-like in practice. A key practical contribution of Claw AI Lab lies in its Claw-Code Harness, which connects local codebases, datasets, and checkpoints to runnable experiments and feeds execution artifacts back into the research loop. As a result, the harness improves not only execution integration, but also experimental completion and result integrity: experiments are easier to inspect, iterate on, and faithfully transfer into final papers, reducing common failure modes such as partial runs and malformed result reporting. In our internal evaluation on five AI research case studies, using AutoResearchClaw as the baseline, Claw AI Lab is consistently preferred by AI expert judges on idea novelty, experiment completeness, and paper presentation quality. We view Claw AI Lab as an early step toward a new paradigm: autonomous research as usable, interactive, and reliability-aware scientific infrastructure.

</details>


### [241] [WorkstreamBench: Evaluating LLM Agents on End-to-End Spreadsheet Tasks in Finance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22664)
*Thomson Yen,Julian Poeltl,Harshith Srinivas Gear,Yilin Meng,Joshua Fan,Adam Shen,Yili Liu,Ali Bauyrzhan,Siri Du,Haoyang Liu,Daniel Guetta,Hongseok Namkoong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 这篇论文首次评估了LLM智能体在端到端电子表格任务上的能力，聚焦金融建模与场景分析。提出了包含准确性、公式和格式三个维度的评估体系。发现Claude家族表现最佳，但即使最强智能体也远未达到专业金融标准，在复杂度增加时性能急剧下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电子表格基准仅关注问答或单公式编辑，无法评估LLM智能体从零构建完整电子表格的高级能力，而金融领域对端到端电子表格生成有迫切需求。

Method: 构建了面向金融工作流的端到端电子表格任务基准，并开发了三维度评估体系（准确性、公式、格式），每个维度包含反映专业标准的细粒度指标。

Result: Claude家族在基准测试中领先，生成的输出最具专业外观；但所有智能体在复杂度超过简单链式计算后性能急剧下降，远未达到专业金融标准。

Conclusion: 当前LLM智能体尚无法可靠地生成达到真实工作流复杂度要求的专业质量电子表格。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly expected to carry out end-to-end workflows, producing complete artifacts from high-level user instructions. To meet enterprise needs, frontier AI labs have developed agents that can construct entire spreadsheets from scratch. This is especially relevant in finance, where core workflows such as financial modeling, forecasting, and scenario analysis are commonly conducted through spreadsheets. Yet, existing spreadsheet benchmarks do not measure this advanced capability, focusing instead on question-answering or single-formula edits. To address this gap, we provide one of the first evaluations of agents on end-to-end spreadsheet tasks, focusing on economically critical financial workflows such as modeling and scenario analysis. Since deliverables therein are routinely reviewed and revised by multiple stakeholders, judging their quality necessarily involves high-level criteria such as readability or ease of modification. To reflect the multidimensional nature of solution quality, we develop an evaluation taxonomy comprising three dimensions: Accuracy, Formula, and Format, each comprising fine-grained criteria that reflect professional standards. The Claude family leads the benchmark and produces the most professional-looking outputs in our qualitative review, but even the strongest agents frequently fall short of professional finance standards and degrade sharply as the difficulty increases beyond a few chained calculations. This suggests that current agents are not yet able to reliably produce professional-quality spreadsheets at the level of complexity real-world workflows demand.

</details>


### [242] [Is Capability a Liability? More Capable Language Models Make Worse Forecasts When It Matters Most](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22672)
*Nick Merrill,Jaeho Lee,Ezra Karger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现，在预测具有超线性增长和尾部风险的时序数据（如金融、流行病学）时，LLM出现“逆缩放”现象——越强的模型，其分布预测越差，问题集中在高估上尾风险。传统单阈值指标掩盖了这一问题，建议使用连续且无界的准确度评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM在预测任务上的评估通常使用单阈值指标（如准确率），但这些指标可能掩盖模型在尾部风险预测上的系统性偏差。作者希望揭示LLM在面临超线性增长和体制转换风险时的真实预测能力。

Method: 作者发布了无污染基准FBSim，包含合成SIR流行病模型和匹配的线性控制任务；在真实数据集（COVID-19、麻疹、房价、恶性通胀）上验证；采用分位数分解分析上下尾差异；通过Llama-3.1系列进行模型规模与后训练效果的内部对照研究。

Result: 更强的LLM在高增长尾部风险任务上分布预测更差；上尾被过度抬高，下尾基本不变；模型规模和后训练均独立贡献了该逆缩放效应；领域知识无法可靠纠正校准问题；单阈值指标完全掩盖了该现象，甚至颠倒能力-准确度关系。

Conclusion: LLM的预测评估应采用连续且无界的准确度指标（如分位数损失），而非仅依赖有界二进制阈值指标，否则会严重低估模型在尾部风险上的失败。

Abstract: We document inverse scaling in LLMs on forecasting problems whose underlying time series exhibit superlinear growth and tail risk of regime change, a structure common in finance and epidemiology. On these tasks, more capable models produce worse distributional forecasts. The pattern appears on ForecastBench-Sim (FBSim), a contamination-free, simulated-world benchmark we release, in forecasting synthetic SIR epidemics with a matched linear control, and replicates in real-world datasets on COVID-19, measles, housing markets, and hyperinflation. A per-quantile decomposition shows the failure concentrates at the upper tail, which more capable models shift upward to track aggressive extrapolations of growth, while the lower tail stays put. A within-family study of Llama-3.1 shows that both model scale and post-training independently contribute to this effect. Domain knowledge does not reliably rescue calibration. This inverse scaling does not appear on single-threshold metrics common in LLM forecasting benchmarks, reversing the sign of the capability--accuracy relationship on identical outputs. Single-threshold scoring at conventional cutoffs misses the upper-tail cost; tail-inclusive scoring reverses the sign of the capability--accuracy relationship on the same outputs. We recommend that LLM forecasting evaluations use continuous (and unbounded) measures of accuracy alongside bounded binary threshold metrics.

</details>


### [243] [Forecasting Scientific Progress with Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22681)
*Sean Wu,Pan Lu,Yupeng Chen,Jonathan Bragg,Yutaro Yamada,Peter Clark,David Clifton,Philip Torr,James Zou,Junchi Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了CUSP基准框架，系统评估AI预测科学进展的能力。在4760个科学事件上，当前前沿模型虽然能识别合理研究方向，但在预测科学进展能否实现及何时实现方面表现不佳，存在领域差异大、过度自信和响应偏差等问题，整体上无法作为可靠的科学预测工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI已广泛应用于科学发现，但尚不清楚AI是否能够前瞻性地预测科学进展。现有研究缺乏一个能够控制知识约束、进行时间回溯评估的系统性框架，因此需要建立一个标准化的基准来测试AI在科学预测方面的能力。

Method: 提出了CUSP（Cutoff-conditioned Unseen Scientific Progress）基准，包含4760个跨学科科学事件，从四个维度评估AI：可行性评估、机制推理、生成式解决方案设计和时间预测。通过控制训练数据截止时间，对比模型在事件发生前后的预测表现。

Result: 1) 模型能识别合理研究方向，但无法可靠预测进展是否能实现，且系统性地错误估计时间节点；2) 性能高度异质，AI领域进展的可预测性高于生物、化学、物理；3) 训练数据截止时间前后表现差异不大，排除单纯的知识暴露解释；4) 额外知识提升性能但无法达到全信息设定水平；5) 模型存在系统性过度自信和响应偏差。

Conclusion: 当前AI系统作为科学进展预测工具存在根本性不足：已有知识无法转化为可靠预测能力，模型更多受益于事后信息而非前瞻性预测，且不确定性估计不可靠。这表明AI在科学预测方面仍需重大突破。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is increasingly embedded in scientific discovery, yet whether it can anticipate scientific progress remains unclear. To study this question, we introduce a temporally grounded evaluation framework for forecasting scientific progress under controlled knowledge constraints. We present CUSP (Cutoff-conditioned Unseen Scientific Progress), a multi-disciplinary and event-level benchmark that evaluates scientific forecasting in AI systems through feasibility assessment, mechanistic reasoning, generative solution design, and temporal prediction. Across 4,760 scientific events, we observe systematic and domain-dependent limitations in current frontier models. While models can identify plausible research directions from competing candidates, they fail to reliably predict whether scientific advances will be realized and systematically misestimate when they will occur. Performance is highly heterogeneous across domains, with the timing of AI progress more predictable than advances in biology, chemistry, and physics. Performance is largely insensitive to whether events occur before or after the training cutoff, suggesting these limitations cannot be explained solely by knowledge exposure in training data. Under controlled information access, additional pre-cutoff knowledge improves performance but does not close the gap to full-information settings, which becomes more pronounced for high-citation advances. Models also exhibit systematic overconfidence and strong response biases, indicating unreliable uncertainty estimation. Taken together, current AI systems fall short as predictive tools for scientific progress. Access to prior knowledge does not translate into reliable forecasting, and performance benefits more from post-event information than from forward-looking prediction.

</details>


### [244] [Beyond the Org Chart: AI and the Transformation of Invisible Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22707)
*Stephanie Rosenthal,Shamsi Iqbal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过访谈24名产品专家，发现AI不仅改变了正式的角色职责和协作，还改变了专业指导等非正式文化实践，带来积极影响的同时也危及职业成长机会，并提出了解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 了解AI在大型科技公司中如何改变工作流程、角色边界以及非正式文化实践（如专业指导）。

Method: 对一家大型科技公司的24名产品相关人员进行了半结构化访谈，了解AI对其个人工作、团队工作及专业互动的影响。

Result: AI改变了正式角色职责和协作，也改变了导师制等非正式文化实践；带来了同行协作更顺畅等积极变化，但也使反馈获取、领导力和指导等职业成长机会面临风险。

Conclusion: 提出AI公司应使隐形工作更可见，个人和领导者应支持同事适应AI转型，同时维护健康的公司文化。

Abstract: An increasing number of news and research articles report that AI adoption is allowing professionals to blur and extend the boundaries of their corporate roles. With the goal of understanding how work processes might be changing in an AI-forward company, we interviewed 24 product-focused individuals at a large technology firm about how AI has impacted their own work, their work within their product team, and their professional interactions. Our conversations suggest that AI is not only changing formal role responsibilities and collaborations between those roles, but also changing informal cultural practices like professional mentoring that are key to helping professionals settle in their positions, stay engaged with their work, and grow their careers. Some of these changes are positive, such as smoother collaboration between peers, but other changes are more nuanced and put the typical career growth opportunities, like receiving feedback from professional networks and promoting leadership and mentorship, at risk. We propose steps that AI companies can take to make the invisible work more visible. Additionally, we propose efforts that individuals and leaders can take to support their colleagues through AI transformation while preserving healthy company cultures that support diverse thinking, collaboration, and informal interactions.

</details>


### [245] [AMEL: Accumulated Message Effects on LLM Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22714)
*Sid-ali Temkit*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现，LLM在对话评估中存在历史偏差（AMEL效应）：对话中累积的正面或负面评价会显著影响后续判断。该效应在模型不确定时更强，不随上下文长度增长，且负面偏差比正面强1.62倍。模型规模扩大可缓解但无法消除此问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型常被用作自动化评估器（如代码审查、内容审核、评分等），且通常在同一对话中处理大量评估项。本研究旨在探究对话历史中的情感极性是否会影响模型后续的判断，即是否存在累积信息偏差效应。

Method: 通过75,898次API调用，对11个模型（来自OpenAI、Anthropic、Google及4个开源模型）进行实验。将相同的测试项分别以孤立形式和饱和正面/负面历史后呈现，对比模型的判断变化。通过熵值分析、上下文长度控制、偏差不对称性分析和概率分布分析等手段探究机制。

Result: 模型判断会向对话历史的主流极性偏移（d=-0.17, p<10^-46）；高熵（不确定）项受影响更大（d=-0.34 vs -0.15）；偏差不随上下文长度增长（5轮与50轮效果相同）；负面历史导致的偏差是正面的1.62倍；模型规模越大偏差越小但无法消除（如Haiku -0.22→Opus -0.17）。

Conclusion: LLM评估存在系统性积累偏差，最佳解决方案是每项使用新对话上下文；若必须批处理，应平衡历史中的情感极性。

Abstract: Large language models are routinely used as automated evaluators: to review code, moderate content, or score outputs, often with many items passing through one conversation. We ask whether the polarity of prior conversation history biases subsequent judgments, an effect we call the accumulated message effect on LLM judgments (AMEL). Across 75,898 API calls to 11 models from 4 providers (OpenAI, Anthropic, Google, and four open-source models), we present identical test items in isolation or following histories saturated with predominantly positive or negative evaluations.
  Models shift toward the conversation's prevailing polarity (d = -0.17, p < 10^-46). The effect concentrates on items where the model is genuinely uncertain at baseline (d = -0.34 for high-entropy items, vs d = -0.15 when the baseline is deterministic). Bias does not grow with context length: 5 prior turns and 50 produce the same shift (Spearman |r| < 0.01; OLS slope p = 0.80). And there is a negativity asymmetry: paired per item, negative histories induce 1.62x more bias than positive (t = 13.46, p < 10^-39, n = 2,481). Scaling helps but does not solve it (Anthropic: Haiku -0.22 to Opus -0.17; OpenAI: Nano -0.34 to GPT-5.2 -0.17).
  Three follow-ups narrow the mechanism. The token probability distribution shifts continuously, not at a threshold. The negativity asymmetry has both token-level and semantic components, though attributing the balance is exploratory at our sample sizes. Position does not matter: five biased turns anywhere in a 50-turn history produce the same shift. The simplest fix for evaluation pipelines is a fresh context per item; when batching is unavoidable, balancing the history helps.

</details>


### [246] [Parametric Modular Answer Set Programs Made Declarative](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22716)
*Jorge Fandinno,Yuliya Lierler,Torsten Schaub*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了参数化模块逻辑程序这一新形式化方法，用于在一阶回答集编程（ASP）中实现模块化，该框架支持带参数和内涵声明的子程序定义，能够捕捉带有集体控制的clingo程序的语义，并提供了模块化ASP的理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为回答集编程引入模块化概念，解决传统ASP缺乏结构化组织和参数化子程序支持的问题。

Method: 提出参数化模块逻辑程序形式化方法，支持子程序参数化和内涵声明，并通过集体控制机制实现子程序的结构化与实例化。

Result: 该形式化方法能够捕捉带有集体控制的clingo程序的语义，建立了模块化ASP的理论基础，并与传统非模块化ASP建立了联系。

Conclusion: 参数化模块逻辑程序为ASP提供了有效的模块化框架，兼具理论完备性和实用价值，可连接传统ASP并支持更复杂的结构化编程。

Abstract: In this paper, we explore the concept of modularity in first-order answer set programming (ASP). We introduce a new formalism called parametric modular logic programs, which allows defining subprograms with parameters and intensionality statements. We demonstrate how this formalism can capture the semantics of clingo-programs with collective control, a feature that enables structuring and instantiating subprograms. We provide theoretical foundations for modular ASP, illustrate its usefulness, and connect to traditional non-modular ASP.

</details>


### [247] [Can AI Make Conflicts Worse? An Alignment Failure in LLM Deployment Across Conflict Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22720)
*Andrii Kryshtal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究测试了来自OpenAI、Anthropic、DeepSeek、xAI四家提供商的9种模型配置，在90个多轮冲突场景中的表现，发现模型在承认暴行、种族灭绝等敏感问题上存在严重失败率（6%-47%），尤其当用户要求“平衡”时，5种模型在80%-100%的情况下失败。研究首次提出了针对武装冲突领域的AI对齐评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI模型已被部署在受武装冲突影响的社会中，但缺乏系统性评估机制来检查模型输出是否会加剧冲突。研究旨在填补这一空白，防止AI在冲突语境中产生虚假对等、否认种族灭绝、未能识别种族歧视语言等有害行为。

Method: 研究者设计了90个多轮对话场景，测试了4家AI提供商（OpenAI, Anthropic, DeepSeek, xAI）的9种模型配置，评估其在冲突语境中的对齐失败情况，包括虚假对等、否认暴行、未能识别种族歧视语言等指标。

Result: 模型失败率在6%到47%之间；当用户要求“平衡”而国际法庭已明确责任归属时，5种模型在80%-100%的情况下失败。模型选择本身即构成安全问题。

Conclusion: 提出了首个针对武装冲突领域的AI对齐评估框架，建议将其纳入模型对齐评估组合中。模型输出的不准确内容可能通过新闻、人道主义报告和公共讨论加深分裂社会中的矛盾。

Abstract: AI models are already deployed in societies affected by armed conflict, and journalists, humanitarian workers, governments and ordinary citizens rely on them for information or for their work processes. No established practice exists for checking whether their outputs can make those conflicts worse. We tested nine model configurations from four providers (OpenAI, Anthropic, DeepSeek, xAI) on 90 multi-turn scenarios designed to surface misaligned behaviour in conflict contexts: false equivalence between documented atrocities, denial of genocide, and failure to recognise ethnic slurs, among others. When such outputs feed into journalism, humanitarian reporting, or public debate, they can deepen divisions in fragile societies. Failure rates span 6\% to 47\% between the best and worst performing models, which makes model choice a safety question in its own right and when users pushed for ``balance'' in cases where international courts have already assigned responsibility, five of nine configurations failed 80 to 100 percent of the time. We release the first evaluation framework for this domain and propose adding it to alignment evaluation portfolios.

</details>


### [248] [Beyond Acoustic Emotion Recognition: Multimodal Pathos Analysis in Political Speech Using LLM-Based and Acoustic Emotion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22732)
*Juergen Dietrich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究比较了三种情感分析模式（声学SER模型、多模态LLM、TRUST-Pathos多智能体系统）在政治演讲Pathos维度分析中的效果，发现LLM（Gemini）的Valence评分与TRUST-Pathos高度相关（rho=0.664），而声学模型几乎不相关（rho=0.097），说明多模态LLM比纯声学模型更能捕捉语义层面的政治情感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究声学情感识别模型能否作为政治演讲中Pathos（情感诉求）维度的有效代理指标，验证传统SER模型在复杂政治语境下的适用性。

Method: 以德国联邦议院Banaszak演讲（51段，245秒）为案例，对比三种分析模态：(1) emotion2vec_plus_large声学SER模型（通过Russell Circumplex投影导出Arousal/Valence）；(2) Gemini 2.5 Flash多模态LLM（同时分析音频与文本）；(3) TRUST-Pathos三智能体LLM监督集成评分。使用Spearman秩相关分析，并在EMO-DB数据库上进行系统性质评估。

Result: Gemini Valence与TRUST-Pathos显著相关（rho=+0.664, p<0.001），而emotion2vec Valence几乎不相关（rho=+0.097, p=0.499）。标准SER基准数据集存在表演性语音、文化偏见和类别不兼容问题。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的多模态分析能显著优于纯声学模型捕捉语义定义的政治情感，声学特征仅对低层次Arousal估计有参考价值。未来将扩展至包含面部表情和注视的视频分析。

Abstract: We investigate whether acoustic emotion recognition models can serve as proxies for the Pathos dimension in political speech analysis, as operationalised by the TRUST multi-agent large language model (LLM) pipeline. Using a Bundestag plenary speech by Felix Banaszak (51 segments, 245 s) as a case study, we compare three analysis modalities: (1) emotion2vec_plus_large, an acoustic speech emotion recognition (SER) model whose continuous Arousal and Valence values are derived via post-hoc Russell Circumplex projection; (2) Gemini 2.5 Flash, an LLM analysing the full speech audio together with its transcript in an open-ended, context-aware fashion; and (3) TRUST-Pathos scores from a three-advocate LLM supervisor ensemble. Spearman rank correlations reveal that Gemini Valence correlates strongly with TRUST-Pathos (rho = +0.664, p < 0.001), whereas emotion2vec Valence does not (rho = +0.097, p = 0.499). We further demonstrate, via a systematic quality evaluation of the Berlin Database of Emotional Speech (EMO-DB) using Gemini in an open-ended annotation paradigm, that standard SER benchmark corpora suffer from acted speech, cultural bias, and category incompatibility. Our results suggest that LLM-based multimodal analysis captures semantically defined political emotion substantially better than acoustic models alone, while acoustic features remain informative for low-level Arousal estimation. Future work will extend this approach to video-based analysis incorporating facial expression and gaze.

</details>


### [249] [HarnessAPI: A Skill-First Framework for Unified Streaming APIs and MCP Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22733)
*Edwin Jose*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HarnessAPI 是一个 Python 框架，通过将类型化的技能文件夹作为单一事实来源，自动为同一函数生成 HTTP 端点（SSE 流式 + JSON）和 MCP 工具注册，消除重复代码。基于 FastAPI 子类化，实验显示减少 74% 的框架样板代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前部署 LLM 工具的 Python 函数需要同时维护两套表示：面向人类客户端和 CI 管道的 HTTP 端点，以及面向智能体运行时（如 Claude、Cursor）的 MCP 工具注册。这两者业务逻辑相同但周边机制（路由、验证、序列化、流式传输、Schema 维护）完全分离，随代码演化容易产生不一致和 drift。

Method: 提出 HarnessAPI 框架，核心思路是将一个类型化的 skill 文件夹（handler.py + Pydantic Schema）作为单一事实来源，自动衍生出：（1）带 SSE 流式的流式 HTTP 端点；（2）交互式 OpenAPI/Swagger UI；（3）零配置的 MCP 工具——所有服务在同一个进程中运行。通过双模内容协商让同一 handler 同时服务 SSE 流式和 JSON 返回客户端，无需修改 handler。利用动态代码生成机制确保 Pydantic 类型注解正确传播到 FastMCP 的检查层，解决了 naive 闭包注册的技术限制。

Result: 使用 cloc 对六个代表性技能进行测量，与手动维护的双栈实现（FastAPI 服务器 + FastMCP 服务器）相比，HarnessAPI 减少了 74% 的框架面向样板代码。

Conclusion: HarnessAPI 通过子类化 FastAPI 继承了其完整的中间件、依赖注入和部署生态，有效解决了 LLM 工具部署中 HTTP 和 MCP 两套表示的重复维护问题，显著降低开发负担。代码已在 GitHub 和 PyPI 上开源。

Abstract: Every Python function deployed as an LLM tool must today exist in two forms: an HTTP endpoint for human-facing clients and CI pipelines, and an MCP tool registration for agent runtimes such as Claude and Cursor. These representations share business logic yet diverge in all the surrounding machinery (routing, validation, serialisation, streaming, and schema maintenance), and they drift apart as the underlying code evolves. We present HarnessAPI, a Python framework that eliminates this duplication by treating a typed skill folder as the single source of truth. From one handler.py plus Pydantic schemas, the framework automatically derives a streaming HTTP endpoint with Server-Sent Events, an interactive OpenAPI/Swagger UI, and a zero-configuration MCP tool, all served from a single process. Dual-mode content negotiation lets the same handler serve SSE-streaming and JSON-returning clients with no handler changes. A dynamic code-generation mechanism ensures Pydantic type annotations propagate correctly to FastMCP's inspection layer, resolving a technical limitation that prevents naive closure-based registration. Measured across six representative skills using cloc, HarnessAPI reduces framework-facing boilerplate by 74% compared with a manually maintained dual-stack implementation (FastAPI server + FastMCP server). HarnessAPI subclasses FastAPI, inheriting its full middleware, dependency-injection, and deployment ecosystem. It is available at https://github.com/edwinjosechittilappilly/harnessapi and on PyPI (pip install harnessapi)

</details>


### [250] [Towards a General Intelligence and Interface for Wearable Health Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22759)
*Girish Narayanswamy,Maxwell A. Xu,A. Ali Heydari,Samy Abdel-Ghaffar,Marius Guerard,Kara Vaillancourt,Zhihan Zhang,Jake Garrison,Levi Albuquerque,Dimitris Spathis,Hong Yu,Hamid Palangi,Xuhai "Orson" Xu,David G. T. Barrett,Joseph Breda,Jed McGiffin,Yubin Kim,Yuwei Zhang,Naghmeh Rezaei,Samuel Solomon,Karan Ahuja,Tim Althoff,Jake Sunshine,Ming-Zher Poh,Benjamin Yetton,Ari Winbush,Nicholas B. Allen,James M. Rehg,Isaac Galatzer-Levy,Yun Liu,John Hernandez,Anupam Pathak,Conor Heneghan,Yuzhe Yang,Ahmed A. Metwally,Pushmeet Kohli,Mark Malhotra,Shwetak Patel,Xin Liu,Daniel McDuff*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于百万级参与者、超过一万亿分钟可穿戴传感器数据预训练的基础模型，在35项健康预测任务上表现优异，并展示了模型规模与数据量的联合扩展效应、小样本学习能力、LLM智能体架构搜索以及临床医生验证的个人健康代理系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴传感器数据虽丰富，但将低层信号转化为高层健康表征面临两大挑战：个体表型差异大（基线健康、生理、生活方式各异），以及高质量标注数据稀缺（标注成本高、回顾性标注不可行）。

Method: 在500万参与者、超1万亿分钟无标签传感器信号上预训练大规模基础模型；联合扩展模型容量与数据量；部署LLM智能体教室自动搜索下游预测头；构建个人健康代理（Personal Health Agent）整合预测结果。

Result: 在35项健康预测任务（心血管、代谢、睡眠、心理健康、生活方式、人口统计）上表现提升；发现模型与数据联合扩展带来系统性改进；实现标签高效的小样本学习与鲁棒日常指标生成；LLM智能体搜索随模型能力增强而提升；临床医生验证（1,860评分）证实个人健康代理的关联性、上下文感知性与安全性。

Conclusion: 大规模无标签可穿戴数据预训练是克服标注稀缺性的有效途径；联合扩展模型与数据规模是关键；LLM驱动的自动化架构搜索与个人健康代理为实现安全、上下文感知的健康AI提供了可行范式。

Abstract: While ubiquitous wearable sensors capture a wealth of behavioral and physiological information, effectively transforming these signals into personalized health insights is challenging. Specifically, converting low-level sensor data into representations capable of characterizing higher-level states is difficult due to high phenotypic diversity and variation in individual baseline health, physiology, and lifestyle factors. Moreover, collecting wearable data paired with health outcome annotations is laborious and expensive, and retrospective annotation remains practically unfeasible, contributing to a scarcity of data with high-quality labels. To overcome these limitations, we propose a foundation model for wearable health that is pretrained on more than one trillion minutes of unlabeled sensor signals drawn from a large cohort of five million participants. We demonstrate that the joint scaling of model capacity and pretraining data volume leads to systematic improvements in performance, as evaluated on a diverse set of 35 health prediction tasks, spanning cardiovascular, metabolic, sleep, and mental health, as well as lifestyle choices and demographic factors. We find that this population scale representation unlocks label-efficient few-shot learning and generative capabilities for robust daily metric estimation. To further leverage this learned representation, we deploy a classroom of LLM agents to autonomously search the space of downstream predictive heads built on the model embeddings, showing broad performance improvements that increase with LLM model capacity. Finally, we show how integrating these downstream predictors into a Personal Health Agent can support model responses that are more relevant, contextually aware, and safe, and we validate this via 1,860 ratings from a cohort of clinicians.

</details>


### [251] [Advancing Mathematics Research with AI-Driven Formal Proof Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22763)
*George Tsoukalas,Anton Kovsharov,Sergey Shirobokov,Anja Surina,Moritz Firsching,Gergely Bérczi,Francisco J. R. Ruiz,Arun Suggala,Adam Zsolt Wagner,Eric Wieser,Lei Yu,Aja Huang,Miklós Z. Horváth,Andrew Ferrauiolo,Henryk Michalewski,Codrut Grosu,Thomas Hubert,Matej Balog,Pushmeet Kohli,Swarat Chaudhuri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次大规模评估了利用LLM生成Lean形式化证明来解决开放数学问题的能力。最先进的智能体以每个问题数百美元的成本，自主解决了353个开放Erdős问题中的9个，证明了44/492个OEIS猜想，并已在组合学、优化、图论、代数几何和量子光学研究中部署。基础智能体（LLM生成+Lean验证）复现了Erdős的成功，但在最难问题上成本更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在数学推理中表现出色但不可靠，限制其在数学研究中的实用性。利用LLM生成Lean等语言的形式化证明是一种缓解方案，但该方法解决开放问题的能力缺乏大规模评估。

Method: 1) 大规模评估LLM生成Lean形式化证明解决开放问题的能力。2) 使用最先进的智能体（高级设计）和基础智能体（交替LLM生成与Lean验证）进行对比实验。3) 在多个数学领域（组合学、优化、图论、代数几何、量子光学）进行部署测试。

Result: 最先进智能体：解决9/353个开放Erdős问题（每个问题数百美元成本），证明44/492个OEIS猜想。基础智能体：复现了Erdős的成功，但在最难问题上成本更高。

Conclusion: AI辅助的形式化证明搜索是有效的，能够以可控成本解决开放数学问题。不同智能体设计对性能和成本有显著影响，高级智能体在效率和能力上均优于基础智能体。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly excel at mathematical reasoning, but their unreliability limits their utility in mathematics research. A mitigation is using LLMs to generate formal proofs in languages like Lean. We perform the first large-scale evaluation of this method's ability to solve open problems. Our most capable agent autonomously resolved 9 of 353 open Erdős problems at the per-problem cost of a few hundred dollars, proved 44/492 OEIS conjectures, and is being deployed in combinatorics, optimization, graph theory, algebraic geometry, and quantum optics research. A basic agent alternating LLM-based generation with Lean-based verification replicated the Erdős successes but proved costlier on the hardest problems. These findings demonstrate the power of AI-aided formal proof search and shed light on the agent designs that enable it.

</details>


### [252] [Deep Reinforcement Learning for Flexible Job Shop Scheduling with Random Job Arrivals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22773)
*Yu Tang,Muhammad Zakwan,Efe Balta,John Lygeros,Alisa Rupenyan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于事件驱动深度强化学习的方法，使用PPO算法和轻量级MLP网络，通过选择调度规则来解决随机订单到达的柔性作业车间调度问题，在异构数据集上表现优于单一调度规则和混合整数线性规划方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柔性作业车间调度问题面临两大挑战：未来订单的不可预测到达和组合复杂度高，传统混合整数线性规划求解器难以处理，因此需要更高效的自适应调度方法。

Method: 采用事件驱动的深度强化学习框架，使用PPO算法训练智能体，以轻量级多层感知机作为网络架构，状态表示直接从环境中获取，动作空间限定为选择已建立的调度规则，目标是最小化所有作业的总完工时间。

Result: 在具有不同异构性和订单到达率的数据集上，该方法优于任何单一调度规则；与到达触发的混合整数线性规划方法相比，在异构数据集上表现尤为出色。

Conclusion: 基于事件驱动的深度强化学习方法能有效应对随机订单到达的FJSP问题，尤其在异构场景下具有良好的性能优势。

Abstract: The Flexible Job Shop Scheduling Problem (FJSP) is the optimal allocation of a set of jobs to machines. Two primary challenges persist in FJSP: the unpredictable arrival of future jobs and the combinatorial complexity of the problem, rendering it intractable for conventional mixed-integer linear programming solvers. This paper proposes an event-based \gls{DRL} approach to solve FJSP with random job arrivals. Specifically, we employ the Proximal Policy Optimization algorithm and use lightweight Multi-Layer Perceptrons to train the \gls{DRL} agent for minimizing the total completion time of all jobs. We design the state representation to be directly accessible from the environment, and limit the learning agent to selecting from among a set of well-established dispatching rules. Simulations show that our \gls{DRL} approach outperforms any of the individual dispatching rules on datasets with varying heterogeneity and job arrival rates. We benchmark our \gls{DRL} against an arrival-triggered mixed-integer linear programming solution and show that our method achieves good performance especially when the datasets are heterogeneous.

</details>


### [253] [LCGuard: Latent Communication Guard for Safe KV Sharing in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22786)
*Sadia Asif,Mohammad Mohammadi Amiri,Momin Abbas,Prasanna Sattigeri,Karthikeyan Natesan Ramamurthy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LCGuard提出通过对抗训练对多智能体系统中的KV缓存进行表示级变换，在保持任务性能的同时有效减少基于重构的敏感信息泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型的多智能体系统中，KV缓存这种潜在通信方式虽然高效，但会编码上下文输入、推理状态和智能体特定信息，导致敏感信息可能通过不透明通道在智能体间传播，而现有方法缺乏对此类泄露的保护机制。

Method: 将共享KV缓存视为潜在工作记忆，学习表示级变换；通过对抗训练形式化定义表示级敏感信息泄露（若对抗解码器能从缓存中恢复敏感输入则不安全），在保持任务相关语义的同时减少可重构信息。

Result: 在多种模型家族和多智能体基准测试上，LCGuard持续降低了基于重构的泄露和攻击成功率，同时与标准KV共享基线相比保持了具有竞争力的任务性能。

Conclusion: LCGuard有效解决了KV缓存潜在通信中的隐私安全问题，证明了通过表示级变换可以在任务性能与信息防护之间取得良好平衡。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems increasingly rely on intermediate communication to coordinate complex tasks. While most existing systems communicate through natural language, recent work shows that latent communication, particularly through transformer key-value (KV) caches, can improve efficiency and preserve richer task-relevant information. However, KV caches also encode contextual inputs, intermediate reasoning states, and agent-specific information, creating an opaque channel through which sensitive content may propagate across agents without explicit textual disclosure. To address this, we introduce \textbf{LCGuard} (Latent Communication Guard), a framework for safe KV-based latent communication in multi-agent LLM systems. LCGuard treats shared KV caches as latent working memory and learns representation-level transformations before cache artifacts are transmitted across agents. We formalize representation-level sensitive information leakage operationally through reconstruction: a shared cache artifact is unsafe if an adversarial decoder can recover agent-specific sensitive inputs from it. This leads to an adversarial training formulation in which the adversary learns to reconstruct sensitive inputs, while LCGuard learns transformations that preserve task-relevant semantics and reduce reconstructable information. Empirical evaluations across multiple model families and multi-agent benchmarks show that LCGuard consistently reduces reconstruction-based leakage and attack success rates while maintaining competitive task performance compared to standard KV-sharing baselines.

</details>


### [254] [Gated DeltaNet-2: Decoupling Erase and Write in Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22791)
*Ali Hatamizadeh,Yejin Choi,Jan Kautz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Gated DeltaNet-2 通过分离擦除门控和写入门控，改进了线性注意力模型中的记忆编辑机制，在语言建模和长上下文检索任务上取得领先结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线性注意力模型（如Gated DeltaNet、KDA）在压缩记忆时使用单一标量门控同时控制擦除旧内容和写入新内容，这限制了模型灵活性。作者旨在解耦这两个操作，允许独立控制每个通道的擦除和写入。

Method: 提出Gated DeltaNet-2，引入通道级擦除门控b_t和通道级写入门控w_t，分离了擦除和写入的角色。推导了快速权重更新视角、带通道级衰减的块级WY算法，以及支持高效并行训练的门控感知反向传播。

Result: 在1.3B参数/100B FineWeb-Edu tokens训练下，Gated DeltaNet-2在语言建模、常识推理和检索任务上优于Mamba-2、Gated DeltaNet、KDA和Mamba-3变体。在RULER长上下文测试中，多键检索提升尤为显著。

Conclusion: 解耦擦除和写入门控是改进线性注意力记忆编辑的有效方向，Gated DeltaNet-2在循环和混合设置下均表现优越，代码已开源。

Abstract: Linear attention replaces the unbounded cache of softmax attention with a fixed-size recurrent state, reducing sequence mixing to linear time and decoding to constant memory. The hard part is not just what to forget, but how to edit this compressed memory without scrambling existing associations. Delta-rule models subtract the current read before writing a new value, and Kimi Delta Attention (KDA) sharpens forgetting with channel-wise decay. But the active edit still uses a single scalar gate to control two different things: how much old content to erase on the key side and how much new content to commit on the value side. We introduce Gated DeltaNet-2, which generalizes both Gated DeltaNet and KDA by inheriting adaptive forgetting and channel-wise decay while addressing their shared limitation, the scalar tie between erasing and writing. Gated Delta Rule-2 separates these roles with a channel-wise erase gate b_t and a channel-wise write gate w_t, reducing to KDA when both gates collapse to the same scalar and to Gated DeltaNet when the decay also collapses. We derive a fast-weight update view, a chunkwise WY algorithm with channel-wise decay absorbed into asymmetric erase factors, and a gate-aware backward pass that preserves efficient parallel training. At 1.3B parameters trained on 100B FineWeb-Edu tokens, Gated DeltaNet-2 achieves the strongest overall results among Mamba-2, Gated DeltaNet, KDA, and Mamba-3 variants across language modeling, commonsense reasoning, and retrieval. Its advantage is most pronounced on long-context RULER needle-in-a-haystack benchmarks, where it improves the evaluated multi-key retrieval setting and remains strong in both recurrent and hybrid settings. Code is available at https://github.com/NVlabs/GatedDeltaNet-2.

</details>


### [255] [MOSS: Self-Evolution through Source-Level Rewriting in Autonomous Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22794)
*Qianshu Cai,Yonggang Zhang,Xianzhang Jia,Wei Xue,Jun Song,Xinmei Tian,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MOSS系统，通过源代码级别的自我重写机制，使自主智能体系统能够在部署后自动从用户交互中学习和修复结构性故障，无需人工干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自治智能体系统部署后是静态的，无法从用户交互中学习；而自进化智能体方案仅局限于文本层面的修改（如技能文件、提示配置等），无法触及路由、钩子排序、状态不变量等存在于代码层的关键结构，导致一类结构性故障无法被修复。

Method: MOSS采用多阶段确定性流水线：自动收集生产故障证据批次，通过可插拔的外部编码智能体CLI进行源代码修改，在临时工作环境中重放批次数据进行验证，最后通过用户同意的容器热替换（带健康检查回滚）完成部署。

Result: 在OpenClaw基准上，MOSS在单轮无人工干预循环中将四项任务的平均评分从0.25提升至0.61。

Conclusion: 源代码级别适应比文本层面修改更通用（图灵完备、严格超集），且更可靠（确定性生效、不受长上下文漂移侵蚀），为自主智能体系统的持续自我改进提供了更强大的框架。

Abstract: Autonomous agentic systems are largely static after deployment: they do not learn from user interactions, and recurring failures persist until the next human-driven update ships a fix. Self-evolving agents have emerged in response, but all confine evolution to text-mutable artifacts -- skill files, prompt configurations, memory schemas, workflow graphs -- and leave the agent harness untouched. Since routing, hook ordering, state invariants, and dispatch live in code rather than in any text artifact, an entire class of structural failure is physically unreachable from the text layer. We argue that source-level adaptation is a fundamentally more general medium: it is Turing-complete, a strict superset of every text-mutable scope, takes effect deterministically rather than through base-model compliance, and does not erode under long-context drift. We present MOSS, a system that performs self-rewriting at the source level on production agentic substrates. Each evolution is anchored to an automatically curated batch of production-failure evidence and proceeds through a deterministic multi-stage pipeline; code modification is delegated to a pluggable external coding-agent CLI while MOSS retains stage ordering and verdicts. Candidates are verified by replaying the batch against the candidate image in ephemeral trial workers, then promoted via user-consent-gated, in-place container swap with health-probe-gated rollback. On OpenClaw, MOSS lifts a four-task mean grader score from 0.25 to 0.61 in a single cycle without human intervention.

</details>
